# Vehicle → weapons

Which sounding weapon blocks (WEAPONS.md) each vehicle mounts, by `blk:t`; containers resolve one level down to the missile they hold, pylon launchers are dropped and `_default/_user/_ai/_tmc` variants collapse to the core name. Generated by `scripts/vehicle_table.py`.
